the quit screen no longer lies about witnessing stopping. `--retro` one-shot reports journal sessions inside a 6h window with `via=retro`. **ONE detector per cabinet** — beacon and `--headless` claim the same `/tmp/aw-beacon/pid`, so opening the tile beside the PC-agent daemon cannot start a second watcher (two would report the same session seconds apart and the server dedup would not collapse them). **Scout harvesting is GONE** (the round-4b licence-file PII finding): the beacon writes an EVENT log only (`data/witness.log`, 256KB cap) — no tails, no sweeps, no `ps`. `WitnessCore` is now a thin reader of the beacon's `/tmp/aw-beacon/state` so the Status screen shows what the CABINET is doing. **PROVEN THIS SESSION:** cross-build clean (glibc/GLIBCXX/CXXABI compat pass); journal parser validated natively against ChalataLove's REAL corpus — **355/355 records, 0 unparsed timestamps, 0 inverted, truncated-tail safe**, and `parseRfc3339` (hand-rolled: `mktime` would apply the DEVICE's timezone to a stamp that carries its own offset) agrees to the second across `-04:00`/`Z`/`+02:00`; deployed to the owner's Micro via `npm run witness-agent -- start` — `detector start v1.0.0-rc1 source=firmware-log(5.x) paired`, state file ticking at 3s, `--retro` correctly reports 0; and **tier-2 check-in proven from the cabinet against prod** (`{"ok":true,"ts":"2026-09-01 05:30:00"}`, server-stamped). **NEXT: ONE field round then the event.** `tmp/witness-build/arcaid-witness-kit-round5.zip` + `TESTER-INSTRUCTIONS-ROUND5.md` are BUILT and await sending to ChalataLove — the 6.x signal-path detector and the on-cabinet check-in button are the only paths never run on hardware; - **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-30 #132 (SCORE-ENTRY SAFETY + ADMIN SCORE CORRECTION — v2.149.0, branch `feat/score-entry-commas-and-provenance-memory`).** All three parts come out of ONE owner incident: StopNudgingMe submitted `66,661,589,860` for `6,666,158,980` on RTX_Pinball → Daily Grind → World Cup Soccer (`score_history#496`), and by the time it was spotted the table had rotated to COMPLETED — at which point **Arcaid had no score-edit path at any privilege level**, and both available remedies were wrong (DELETE tombstones the WRONG inflated value, drops him to his 3.478B and hands the win to ChalataLove at 4.72B; every submit path requires `games.status = 'ACTIVE'` so there was no way back in). **(1) GROUPED SCORE ENTRY + MAGNITUDE ECHO** — new `admin-ui/src/lib/scoreInput.ts`; the sheet's `score` state now holds the FORMATTED string so `lib/caretEdit`'s splice helpers and `selectionStart` index the same thing the player sees, every numeric read goes through `digitsOnly`, and caret positions cross a re-group as a DIGIT COUNT (inserting a digit into `999,999` also shifts every separator). Desktop moved to `type="text"` — `type="number"` refuses a value containing separators and is the one input type whose `selectionStart` throws. String grouping, not `toLocaleString`: exact past `MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` and locale-stable. **The echo (`6.66 billion`, truncated never rounded) is the half that actually catches an extra digit** — at eleven digits you still have to count comma groups. **(2) PROVENANCE MEMORY** — engine was remembered NOWHERE (device has been global since v2.53.0); adds `arcaid_last_engine` plus a per-game `arcaid_last_provenance:<gameKey>` checked FIRST, keyed on `(roomId, lowercased name)` NOT a game id (a tournament game row is recreated on every rotation, so an id-keyed memory forgets exactly when the table comes round again). Both resolve through the SAME exclusion-filtered option list the dropdown renders — the v2.95.1 regression was a membership test against the unfiltered list. **(3) ADMIN SCORE CORRECTION** — `PATCH /:roomId/score-history/:historyId/score` → `ScoreHistoryService.correctScore`, ADMIN ONLY (the sibling DELETE allows the row's owner; letting a player rewrite their own score is an edit-the-leaderboard tool). Carries the `community_scores` twin + `global_scores` fan-out via the same conservative match rules `deleteEvent` uses (extracted to `findCommunityScoreTwinId`/`findFannedOutGlobalScoreId` so they cannot drift), recomputes `submissions`, invalidates + broadcasts; **no tombstone on the corrected row, no photo delete, and none of the submit-time side effects** (no lobby event, no toast, no notification, nothing announced — a correction is bookkeeping, not a new score). **THE ONE EXCEPTION — a correction DOWNWARD tombstones the OLD value**, because `IScoredApiClient` can submit but cannot edit or delete, so on a mirrored room `ScoreSyncPoller` would re-import the old higher score within a cycle and silently undo the fix; documented cost is that a later genuine score between new and old is suppressed until an admin clears it (Manage Scores → Suppressions). UI = hover pencil beside the trash on GameDetail history rows → dialog using the same grouped input, showing OLD and NEW magnitude side by side. **VERIFIED:** backend `npm run build` clean, `admin-ui npm run build` clean, admin-ui suite 1284/1284, new `src/__tests__/score-correct.test.ts` 15/15, `scoreInput.test.ts` 23/23, keypad suite 12/12. **Local-env note:** `admin-ui/node_modules` had lucide-react 1.27.0 against a `^1.32.0` package.json, so `RotationLogPanel`'s `Broom` import failed to typecheck — pre-existing, fixed locally with `npm install --no-save lucide-react@^1.32.0` (lockfile untouched). **INCIDENT FOLLOW-UPS FOR THE OWNER, both still open:** (a) the prod row itself — the owner is correcting `score_history#496` + `submissions ad7d0665-…-stopnudgingme` + `global_scores 2265a1c6-…` to 6,666,158,980 by hand; once this branch ships that becomes a two-click action; (b) **iScored still holds 66.6B** on the shared RTX board (`iscored_id 106338`) — fix it there too or the poller can re-raise it. **DYNASTY (owner question, answered):** `tournaments.allow_dynasty` = 1 (ON) on ALL FIVE Grind tournaments in prod — it was never enabled; and even OFF it would NOT have stopped a repeat WIN, it only strips the back-to-back winner's PICK rights (`TournamentEngine.isDynastyBlocked` → `pickResolution`), and an explicit disposition (nominate/forfeit) overrides the block anyway. **Benjamin David Cohen's photo-less WCS score (owner question, answered):** `score_history#473`, `source='sync'`, `discord_user_id='iscored:Benjamin David Cohen'`, engine/device `unknown` — iScored poller, and a synced row can NEVER carry a photo (their API returns name+score only). NEXT: open the PR, then back to the desktop VPX witness arc below (#131 Round A + B).

- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-30 #130 (OWNER REQUIREMENT: NEAR-ZERO BARRIER TO ENTRY — download → click install → 2 wizard choices → done. Two ARCHITECTURE CHANGES to D2 as a result; read `tmp/vpx-desktop-witness/FINDINGS-D3-zero-barrier-packaging.md`).** **The decision that makes it possible: BASIC MODE IS THE DEFAULT AND MODIFIES NOTHING.** Tier 1 (process-watch + post-exit `.nv`/VPReg decode) is a complete product alone — install, play, scores land, zero files touched in the user's VPX. **Live mode (the script hook) becomes a ONE-CLICK OPT-IN offered AFTER they've seen it work**, justified by Best-of-5 refereeing + gear-up prevention — we never ask a stranger to let us edit their pinball setup as a precondition. **CHANGE 1 — DROP the in-process `Arcaid.Witness` COM DLL** (D2 Tier 2 assumed one): removes admin/UAC (COM registration), **32/64-bit build matching** (VPX ships x86 AND x64 exes; this rig registers only 64-bit VPinMAME but many users run x86), the AV heuristic that most resembles malware (a DLL loaded into another process), and a whole class of "it didn't register" tickets. Replacement: the hook is **pure VBScript that only appends changed NVRAM bytes to a journal file**; the ordinary user-mode agent does shadow-NVRAM + map decode + upload. **In-VPX footprint = one `arcaid.vbs` + one `ExecuteGlobal` line in `core.vbs`.** **CHANGE 2 — IPC is a JOURNAL FILE the hook appends and the agent tails, deliberately NOT localhost HTTP**: (a) no Windows Firewall prompt during onboarding, (b) **the agent need not be running during play** — the journal accumulates and is ingested later, which is exactly the existing `via=retro` concept (ADR 0021) and kills the "did you start Arcaid first?" failure mode, (c) mirrors the LU cabinet scoreserver's own jsonl-journal design so both witnesses converge. **T7's probe already writes exactly this journal shape, so it validates the production IPC too.** **AUTO-DETECTION VERIFIED (zero questions needed):** `HKCR\VPinMAME.Controller\CLSID` → `InprocServer32` → grandparent = VPX root, confirmed by `scripts\core.vbs` + `tables\` (resolved perfectly on the rig); `WOW6432Node` in parallel reveals bitness. **Self-healing beats getting it right up front** — the always-on process-watch sees every VPX launch's exe path, so it discovers installs the wizard never knew about AND detects our hook being clobbered by a user's VP script-package update (the #1 silent-death scenario) offering a one-click re-patch instead of going dark mid-tournament. **Wizard = 2 decisions/~30s:** install (per-user, no admin, no prereqs) → sign in to Arcaid via browser (reuse `witness_devices` pairing, desktop device type; deferrable — it can journal first and pair later) → "Found Visual Pinball at `<path>`" with basic capture default + a clearly-explained live-mode checkbox → tray icon ("Start with Windows" on by default). Nothing else asked: multiple installs, ROM identification, map management, folder paths all automatic. **Trust surface:** tray shows live proof of life ("Last capture: TFTC 663,929,960, 2 min ago · Live mode on · 131 tables instrumented"), a first-class **"Remove Arcaid from Visual Pinball"** restore-backup button (reversibility is what makes the yes cheap), hook isolated in its own `arcaid.vbs` so the core.vbs change is ONE diffable line, and the hash manifest doubles as an attestation asset. **THE REAL BLOCKERS ARE NOT CODE: (1) SmartScreen/code signing is the #1 adoption killer** — unsigned downloads show "Windows protected your PC" and most users stop; needs an OV/EV cert (~$200–400/yr) + reputation, **budget it before launch**; (2) AV false positives (dropping the DLL removes the worst signature; sign + submit to vendors; keep `ReadProcessMemory` a last resort precisely for this reason); (3) Defender **Controlled Folder Access** can block writes under `Documents` where this rig's VPX lives — detect and explain, never fail silently; (4) VP script-package updates clobbering the hook (self-heal above). **Stack recommendation (to confirm):** logic/protocol in Node/TS reusing `scripts/witness-agent.ts` pairing+reporting so desktop and cabinet share ONE implementation; installer Inno Setup (per-user, no admin) or Velopack for built-in delta auto-update (a stale agent mid-tournament is a support burden); bundle the nvram maps corpus (**ODbL — attribution required in About**) with background refresh; distribute via GitHub Releases + VPUniverse/VPForums threads + Arcaid Discord. **Explicitly NOT required:** Python, Node install, admin, firewall exception, beta VPX, folder picking, ROM config, manual map downloads, or any VPX modification unless opted in. NEXT unchanged: owner runs **T7** (SETUP-T7.md) → then Tier-1 agent build (which IS the shippable basic-mode product).
- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-30 #129 (OWNER RULING: DESKTOP WITNESS TARGETS VPX 10.8.0 ONLY — 10.8.1 is beta/not release-ready. Plugin tier REPLACED by an in-process script hook, and the 10.8.0 live plumbing was VERIFIED to already exist on the rig. Read `tmp/vpx-desktop-witness/FINDINGS-D2-1080-only-design.md`; it supersedes D1 §5 only.).** **The ruling costs TRANSPORT, NOT SIGNAL** — 10.8.1's `PinmameSetMemMap` merely republishes the same pinmame-nvram-maps decode over the plugin bus, and its `OnGameStart/_End` are TABLE-session events our external process-watch already gives free; per-game/per-ball boundaries always came from the maps' `game_state`, which 10.8.0 reaches. **VERIFIED ON THE RIG TODAY: the live-score plumbing already ships in 10.8.0** — VPinMAME **3.7.0.222** exposes `ChangedNVRAM`/`NVRAM`/`RawDmdPixels`/`ChangedLamps`/`GameName`, and shared **`core.vbs` (VPinMAMEDriverVer 3.61) `Sub PinMAMETimer_Timer` line 2457 ALREADY calls `Controller.ChangedNVRAM` every frame and hands deltas to a `NVRAMCallback` hook** (flag `UseVPMNVRAM` line 37, `Dim NVRAMCallback` line 38). So the entire injection is: set the flag before core.vbs loads + `Set NVRAMCallback = GetRef(...)` after. **Coverage measured across the owner's 134 tables: 127 (95%) load shared `controller.vbs`, 131 (98%) use VPinMAME, 0 embed their own core copy (nothing to miss), 0 already use `NVRAMCallback` (no conflict to chain around), only 3 non-VPM (2 are test tables)** — so patching TWO shared files instruments ~98% of the library with zero per-table edits. **Sidecar external `.vbs` CONFIRMED supported in the 10.8.0 exe** (strings `MODIFYING EXTERNAL SCRIPT: ` / `Save externally loaded .vbs script also to .vpx table?`); `vpxtool` v0.33.9 downloaded for extract/import. Still decisive: `VPinMAME.Controller` + `B2S.Server` are InprocServer32 → in-process is MANDATORY for anything live. **Revised stack: Tier 1 external observer agent UNCHANGED (process-watch `-Play` + post-exit `.nv`/VPReg parse — no VPX modification, version-independent, the tamper-resistant timing spine) · Tier 2 = in-proc `Arcaid.Witness` COM helper (shadow NVRAM seeded from `Controller.NVRAM` + `ChangedNVRAM` deltas → map decode → localhost events) driven by a ~3-line hook in shared core.vbs/controller.vbs · Tier 3 originals — now HIGHER value given #128's finding of a second script-side channel (read `superhac/score-server` source first; stay call-compatible with its `VPinball.ScoreServer` API) · reserve: external ReadProcessMemory anchored by .nv pattern-match, zero VPX footprint, spike only if script install proves unacceptable.** Design musts recorded in D2: `ChangedNVRAM` is a DESTRUCTIVE delta read (one consumer, shadow copy mandatory, chain any table callback); all real work in the COM object never VBScript; installer needs backup + hash manifest + one-click revert + a separate `arcaid.vbs` so the core.vbs patch stays 1-2 diffable lines and survives VP script-package updates — **and that hook doubles as an ATTESTATION asset (hash-verify at round start), a stronger integrity story than the plugin would have had.** Define the event schema + localhost wire protocol as the product boundary NOW so a future 10.8.1 plugin is a swappable producer, not a rewrite (same beacon/reporter split as the cabinet). **NEXT = T7, now the decisive test — STAGED, touches nothing in the live install** (`tmp/vpx-desktop-witness/SETUP-T7.md`): a COPY of TFTC + vpxtool-extracted sidecar carrying a 3-block probe that logs live `ChangedNVRAM` deltas + `Controller.NVRAM` baselines to jsonl; `decode-live.py` replays them through the tftc_400 map and prints the score/ball/game-over timeline **plus the one genuine unknown — whether `Controller.NVRAM` array indexes match on-disk `.nv` offsets** (prints an alignment diagnostic either way). Owner runs it: open the copy, play to Game Over (ideally 2 games), quit normally. **T4 (10.8.1 + Inspector) PARKED** — no longer a product path, retained only as a decode oracle. Carried: cabinet RC build, soggybacon round 1, owner HAR scrub, `/release-docs` sweep (2.146–2.148), Dependabot #305/#292.
- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-30 #128 (ChalataLove's STATIC EVIDENCE CORPUS ANALYSED — #126's caveat CLOSED, the launcher's verification rule DECODED, and D1's "originals have no last-game score" consequence FALSIFIED; `tmp/atgames-research/FINDINGS-0s-vpx-evidence-corpus.md` = the read-first doc).** `tmp/witness-build/vpx-score-evidence.zip` returned and analysed (127 files; **extract with `Expand-Archive`, `unzip` refuses the backslash separators**). Corpus: **49 tables · 111 signed game records · 65 leaderboard rows · 355 sessions over 251 distinct tables · six weeks (07-19→08-30) · machine `alp4k-01`**. **(1) CAVEAT CLOSED — natural game-over appends a signed record mid-session, and it is the NORMAL case: PinMAME tables 75 `game_over` vs 1 `shutdown` (98.7%)**; `game_duration` populated on 103/111. Ingestion needn't wait for a table exit — strictly better granularity than AtGames exit-to-submit, so a per-round LIVE feed is on the table. **(2) The launcher's "verified completed game" rule is DECODED and reproducible: a game is verified iff its `game_end.scores` array is non-empty** — all 28 tables with a `leaderboard.json` have ≥1 scored record, all 11 empty-`scores`-only tables have none, and **65/65 leaderboard rows matched a signed `game_end` on (score, timestamp ±90s)**. The leaderboard is a pure derived projection of the signed log. **(3) Coverage limit = the nvram-map corpus, CONFIRMED on independent hardware: 25/25 ROMs that yielded scores have a `game_state` map; 10/11 that yielded `scores:[]` do not** — and the two SAM blanks are `bdk_294`+`trn_174h`, two of the exact five modern Sterns D1 flagged from the OWNER's library. Caveat worth keeping: **`fpwr2_l2` resolves to a GENERIC system7 map, is "covered" per the index, and still produced nothing** — index-derived coverage numbers are optimistic; only ROM-specific maps count. **(4) BIGGEST — NON-PINMAME TABLES DO YIELD LIVE PER-BALL SCORES** (Burner 2026 328,549,040 across 3 timestamped balls; Hellboy; Zissou; TNA; Ice Cold Beer; EM tables 4 Aces/Apache!/Nags — the last three even via natural `game_over`). That CANNOT come from nvram maps, so **a second extraction channel exists** — almost certainly the script-side surface D1 hypothesised for our own Tier 3. **If it lives in the public Windows-capable `superhac/score-server` rather than the closed LU fork, desktop gets the same schema INCLUDING originals and D1's ecosystem gap is already solved in code we can read — READ THAT SOURCE BEFORE DESIGNING TIER 3.** **(5) Live surface confirmed**: `current_scores` carries `players`/`current_player`/**`current_ball`**/live per-player scores mid-play (28 of 49 tables show a mid-game snapshot), plus richer per-table sections than 0r knew — `high_scores`(30) `audits`(15) `mode_champions`(7) `last_played`(6) `adjustments`(5). **(6) SIX INGESTION GOTCHAS (§6 of 0s), each will bite: `accountName` is the CABINET's account not the player's** (the one 2-player game wrote BOTH rows as "Chalatalove" — never attribute players 2+ from it); **`rom` is heterogeneous** (ROM name / free-text display name / sometimes wrong — `vpx-ratfink`→`stest`); **the catalogue-matchable name lives in `vpx-sessions.json` which does NOT join** (keyed by display name, superset at 251 vs 49 — needs time-window correlation, and that join IS the real work); scores are zero-padded at variable widths (1–12 digits); 8 records have `game_duration:0`; 26/355 sessions are sub-60s browse-and-bounce. **(7) Signatures real but UNVERIFIABLE by us**: 111/111 present, every one exactly 64 bytes (Ed25519 shape) — **no pubkey, so today the sig proves nothing on our side**; it is an argument FOR the integration conversation, not a shippable feature. **(8) Privacy**: collector worked (no license/key/token files), but `vpx-launcher.log` carries `legendsID` + a 64-hex `deviceID` — keep in gitignored `tmp/`, never quote the deviceID. **VERDICT: VPX score ingestion is GO on better evidence than expected. migration **167** widened `score_history.source` to `'atgames'` (a full table rebuild) and **168** added `tournaments.atgames_*`. **v2.139.0 (PR #297) — the host controls.** AtGames card on room Settings; Nothing upstream helps and the API structurally cannot see it (AtGames records the exit moment and nothing else), so **P8’s on-device witness is the ONLY mechanism that could ever catch gear-up** — this RAISES P8’s priority. Until then `min_elapsed_sec` is the sole mitigation and is a prompt to look, never a rejection. - **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-18 #77 (PR #242 SHIPPED + PROD-VERIFIED — DISCORD LINK NUDGE, AVATAR PICKER FIXED, SNOWFLAKE LABEL FIXED; supersedes #76).** **PR #242 merged (`14324004e`), deployed, prod-verified** — container recreated on the new image (digest matched), 0 restarts, 0 errors, `/api/version` reports the merge commit. Backup `arcaid.db.pre-nudge-avatar` taken via `VACUUM INTO` and row-verified first. Tests: backend **1874**, admin-ui **856**. **FOUR THINGS SHIPPED.** (1) **Discord link nudge** — the mods asked whether Discord rooms could require Discord sign-in; the owner's first shape was a pop-up at every Google user on join + again on every score submit. **Both halves were wrong and were replaced:** "signed in with Google" is the wrong trigger in BOTH directions (a LINKED Google login canonicalizes to the snowflake and already gets everything; a Discord login sharing no guild gets nothing and is never caught), and re-prompting on submit is nagware for a restriction **that does not exist** — submitting never required Discord, and a warning players learn is false teaches them to ignore the real ones. **~80% already existed from the Discord HQ arc (v2.72.0)**: `DiscordReachabilityService.canDm`, `DmNudgeService`, and the site-wide `NotificationNudgeBanner`. The gap was ONE inverted condition — the banner's onboarding case only fires when a room has NO Discord. `GET /api/me/dm-nudge` now takes an optional `roomId` and folds the room's link status into the SAME response (one request); the server returns null whenever there is nothing honest to say (no integration / already reachable / gateway indeterminate — **uncertainty never nudges**). Two states, two fixes, two messages: `no_discord` → "link your Discord account" → Account Settings; `not_in_guild` → "join the <room> Discord server" → the room's `DISCORD_INVITE_URL` (https-only, so a stored `javascript:` is dropped). Both say plainly "you can keep playing and submitting scores either way". **Also fixed en route: the existing failure banner told Google-only users to "check your Discord privacy settings" — advice that cannot work when they have no Discord linked.** (2) **Two off-switches** — room admin `DISCORD_LINK_REMINDERS` on the Settings page (framed POSITIVELY, default on, `'false'` disables, matching `ROOM_LISTED` so absence = on and no backfill), and a player-facing **"don't remind me again"** checkbox = a PERMANENT per-room opt-out stored server-side (`_discordLinkOptOut` in `user_preferences.notification_prefs`, same underscore-marker idiom as `_dmNudge`, NO migration) — deliberately distinct from the X, which is a 30-day browser-local snooze. Two intentions ("not now" vs "never"), two mechanisms, so a stray X click never silences a room for good. (3) **AVATAR PICKER BUG — owner field report: "I can choose my Discord Avatar but there is no save option. When I exit Account Settings, my profile pic is still my google one."** The save WAS working (prod showed `avatar_preference='discord'` stored). **The nav renders the JWT `avatar` claim cached at login, NOT `user_profiles`** — and while `api/auth.ts`'s REFRESH path already derived it from the profile, the LOGIN paths in `routes/auth.ts` stamped the raw provider picture in, so the choice was invisible everywhere outside Account Settings for up to 24h. New shared `effectiveAvatarUrlFor()` is called before the token mints in BOTH provider blocks — **placed ahead of all THREE branches (super_admin / room_admin / player); the first attempt only covered the player branch, which the owner as super-admin would have missed entirely.** `ViewerAuthContext.setViewerAvatar` patches the cached viewer in place (+localStorage) so it changes immediately, and the picker now says "Applies as soon as you pick one — there's nothing to save" / "Saved". (4) **SNOWFLAKE LABEL BUG (screenshot):** a rotation embed read *"@soggybacon — congrats on the win! **1393376372025458799** handed their pick to mekelburgj."* The cascade was CORRECT (a nominate hand-off into the nominee's queue) — only `labelForPlayer` was wrong: it resolved display_name → iScored alias → raw id, **skipping `user_profiles.username`**, the one rung populated for EVERYONE (written on every login since v2.40.0). Prod check: **2 of 4 active rtx_pinball players (soggybacon, ChalataLove) had neither a display_name nor an alias**, so both leaked their snowflake. Chain is now display_name → username → alias → id, matching `api/auth.ts`'s token mint. **Shipped in PR #239 yesterday and only caught because the owner read a real Discord message — the cascade tests used synthetic ids where the fallback never mattered.** **PROD-VERIFIED by running the SHIPPED compiled code in the container:** all four players resolve to names, and `effectiveAvatarUrlFor` returns DISCORD for mekelburgj / GOOGLE for ChalataLove. **⚠ OWNER ACTION: mekelburgj must sign out + back in** — the current token still carries the pre-fix Google URL; it self-corrects on the next refresh otherwise. **PROD HOUSEKEEPING:** all `/app/data/*.pre-*` backups + `arcaid-precat.db` deleted by the owner (~111MB, disk 45%→44%); post-delete `PRAGMA integrity_check` = **ok**, `foreign_key_check` clean, 152 migrations, live `arcaid.db`/`-shm`/`-wal` intact. **`/app/backups` (17 nightly snapshots, 3.3GB) untouched — that is the real safety net and the real space consumer if space is ever needed.** **NOT BUILT, deliberately:** no gate on submission, no modal; the contextual placements (a note on the Picks page, Discord toggles rendered unavailable in notification settings) are separate surfaces and a natural follow-up. **NEXT ARCS UNCHANGED:** (a) identity claim/link — **DESIGNED NOT BUILT** (`tmp/identity-claim-design.md`), headline is that **`/map-user` self-claim is UNGUARDED** and claimed names now feed the pick cascade; P1 (guarded claim) + P4 (mod queue) ship together because case-only matching sends most real claims to the queue. (b) style-system revamp **P1-remainder** (three-tier picker · editor prune ~61→~30 · real ScoreboardSurface preview + phone toggle · styled font-preview dropdown) → P2 profiles → P3 viewer trim, all pre-GA.

- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-17 #76 (PICK-DELEGATION ARC SHIPPED + PROD-VERIFIED — PR #239, 4 fixes + 3 migrations; supersedes #75).** **PR #239 merged (`04590c8d3`), deployed, prod-verified:** `/api/version` reports the merge commit, the container recreated onto the new image (running digest matched the pulled one — the workflow uses `--remove-orphans`, NOT `--force-recreate`, so this was checked rather than assumed), 0 restarts, 0 errors since boot. Backup `arcaid.db.pre-pick-delegation` taken via `VACUUM INTO` (not `cp` — a live-db file copy can be torn) and row-verified before deploying. **THE BLACKBELT 2018 INCIDENT WAS *TWO* BUGS THAT CANCELLED EACH OTHER OUT.** (1) **Queues were a tournament-global FIFO** — `processSlotMaintenance` took the queue head and never read `picker_discord_id`, so winning activated whoever queued FIRST; and `resolveNextPicker` (the only reader of `picker_dispositions`) was called ONLY in the empty-queue branch, so nominate/forfeit were inert whenever anything was queued. (2) **`finalSyncScoresForGame` had no `deleted_score_suppressions` check** — the poller honoured the tombstone for 2h36m, then deactivation re-imported the deleted score. **ChalataLove was telling the truth:** audit row 113 + `arcaid.log` show he submitted 3,588,843,950 in ARCAID at 00:23:38, Arcaid synced it OUT to iScored 0.2s later, he deleted it at 00:24:42, resubmitted the correct 358,884,390 at 00:25:54 — and the 03:00:03 final sync resurrected the deleted one. **The real winner was soggybacon (723,234,440), and `Magic Castle` (which activated) was soggybacon's own queued pick — the two defects produced the right game for the wrong reason.** **OWNER RULINGS (contract: `tmp/pick-delegation-contract.md`):** a queue is per-player "my pick if I win", never a room play-next list · place cascade = 1st (queue, else window) → 2nd (queue used IMMEDIATELY, else window) → 3rd (**QUEUE ONLY** — no window, no disposition) → auto-pick · a forfeit ALWAYS advances (the stated "nominee-forfeits-and-was-runner-up → auto-pick" shortcut was UNIFIED away) · disposition lifetime is **SPLIT**: `nominate` one-shot, `forfeit`/`auto` standing · NEW `auto` ("roll the dice") disposition. **Also shipped:** `prompt=none` on the 4 LOGIN flows (Discord re-shows its authorization screen on EVERY sign-in without it — deliberately NOT on AccountSettings' `link:` flow, where that screen IS the account chooser) · **avatar provider picker** (`PlayerAvatar` resolves `avatarUrl ?? avatarHash`, so dual-linked users had their Discord avatar permanently buried by Google; migration 151 splits the column semantics so `avatar_url` = EFFECTIVE and ~25 read sites need no change, and both OAuth callbacks now write their own provider store then re-derive). **PROD DATA:** soggybacon's forfeit row cleared (owner-authorised — it was set under one-shot semantics and the split ruling would have silently promoted it to standing). **All 3 migrations dry-run against prod copies BEFORE landing**; post-deploy verified: `queue_order` 1..N across all 3 per-player queues, ChalataLove's Xena now position 1, **0 users' avatars changed**, picker offered only to mekelburgj + ChalataLove. Tests: backend **1855**, admin-ui **846**. **⚠ NEXT ARC — identity claim/link: DESIGNED, NOT BUILT (`tmp/identity-claim-design.md`).** Headline: **`/map-user` self-claim is UNGUARDED** — any guild member can claim any unclaimed iScored name, and claimed names now feed the pick cascade. Owner rulings: near-match = **case-insensitive EXACT only** (no separators, no spaces) · max **3** aliases per account · auto-match against Arcaid username, display name, held aliases, linked Discord username, linked Google local-part · **no mod gate on photo verification** (owner's premise VERIFIED in code: no score is mod-gated today, photos are public via `ScorePhotoModal`, and report routes already exist — so requiring a mod would make claimed scores STRICTER than ordinary ones) · P1 (guarded claim) + P4 (mod queue) ship TOGETHER, because case-only matching sends most real claims to the queue. Still open: retroactive promotion or forward-only? · what actually counts as photo-verified (ChalataLove's row already carries a photo OF THE WRONG NUMBER, so "photo_url is not null" cannot be the test) · rooms without `REQUIRE_SCORE_PHOTO` may not fan out to Global (inert today — rtx_pinball is the only contributor and already requires photos). **ALSO ANSWERED THIS SESSION:** there is NO mechanism to require Discord login or block Google submissions — `REQUIRE_DISCORD_LOGIN` was retired wholesale in v2.80.0 (migration 138) and **`requireDiscordUser` is a MISNOMER** that accepts any provider. The right shape is NOT "require Discord login" (both under- and over-inclusive for guild verification, and it does not guarantee DM delivery either) but **"has a linked Discord identity in your guild"** — the machinery already exists (`isMemberOfGuild`, identity-link-aware canonical ids where a linked Google login's id IS the snowflake, `DiscordReachabilityService`); approval rooms + `AUTO_APPROVE_GUILD_MEMBERS` may already cover it at the membership layer, which is the better place to gate. **ROADMAP ADD:** delete-on-iScored when a score is deleted in Arcaid — **ADR 0011's "no per-score delete API" premise is WRONG** (iScored exposes `deleteScore`/`editScore` via a `data-topscoreid` handle); tombstones are a workaround for a now-solvable problem.

- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-17 #75 (ATGAMES ARC SHIPPED + PROD-VERIFIED — 4 MORE PRs; NEXT SESSION STARTS WITH THE PICK-DELEGATION BUG; supersedes #74).** **Four PRs shipped and deployed tonight, all v2.112.0: #235 #236 #237 #238.** Prod = commit `aa16119ed`. **#235** source attribution reads `atgames_id` not `imported_from` (123 of 279 rows displayed "manual"; the admin source filter also returned 71 of 279, hiding 208) — `imported_from` deliberately NOT rewritten, it records ORIGIN and 85 rows genuinely came from VPS. **#236** `dedup_name` (strips `Williams™ Pinball:` prefixes + `(Natural History)`/`(Dr. Seuss)` series labels for the step-4 lookup; row still STORED under AtGames' name) + `original_work` guard. **`(Pinball)` is NEVER stripped — owner-taught: AtGames ships a pinball TABLE and an emulated arcade ROM of the same licence, different games on different engines. Test-pinned.** **#237** tightened `original_work`: it may merge ONLY onto a row with a BLANK manufacturer. First version reused `isVirtualOnlyManufacturer`, which counts `Original` as virtual-only, so AtGames' TMNT skipped both real machines and landed on a VPX community remix instead — the rule is now inverted (allow-one-known-good, not block-known-bad) so an unforeseen manufacturer value cannot slip past. **#238** `MFG_SPELLING_ALIASES` (`zacarria`→`zaccaria`) for identity comparison ONLY — the VPXS Wizard README misspells it and the owner cannot fix the source, so the row re-forked every sync; a test caught the better property that once the rows MEET, upsert's supplied-wins write REPAIRS the stored spelling. **PROD DATA REPAIRS (all backed up, all 0 scores moved):** 51 merges (29 realpin-wins + 21 AtGames-wins + strips) · TMNT split off Data East 1991 then re-synced (`1 created`) so AtGames' original stands alone · Space Invaders (Bally 1980) + TMNT (Stern 2020) atgames tags stripped — owner: AtGames' versions are NOT the same table · Time Machine typo row merged, then `atgames_id 50256` MOVED off `Time Machine [LTD do Brasil 1984]` onto `Time Machine (Zaccaria, 1983)` · **Combat/Battle family consolidated: 8 merged, 5 renamed, 5 description notes** → `Combat (Battle)`, `Combat 2017 (Battle 2016)`, `Combat Deluxe (Battle Deluxe)`, `Combat Retro (Battle Retro)`, `Combat EM+ (Battle EM+)`; pre-rename names backfilled into `dedup_aliases` so nothing re-forks. Owner rulings: physical machine's name wins, Steam's 2017 not AtGames' 2016, both names in the title (which ALSO makes search work under either name — **`GlobalGameService.search` matches `name LIKE ?` ONLY and never reads the `aliases` column**). **⚠ NEXT SESSION STARTS HERE — PICK-DELEGATION BUG (live, player-visible, investigated but NOT fixed).** Owner reported it as "wrong winner announced"; the DB says otherwise and **three of the four premises were wrong**: ChalataLove genuinely won Blackbelt 2018 (3,588,843,950 vs soggybacon's 723,234,440) so the winner label and congrats were CORRECT, and soggybacon's disposition row is **forfeit with no nominee** (a different user, `698435672586846228`, nominated ChalataLove). **THE REAL DEFECT: the queue is a tournament-wide FIFO** (`ORDER BY queue_order ASC, rowid ASC`, `TournamentEngine.ts:784`) so winning activates whoever's game is at the FRONT — ChalataLove won but soggybacon's `Magic Castle` activated while ChalataLove's own queued `Xena` was skipped. **AND `resolveNextPicker` — which reads/consumes `picker_dispositions` — is only called in the `else` branch where the queue is EMPTY (`TournamentEngine.ts:1428`), so the entire nominate/forfeit feature is INERT whenever anything is queued.** Both disposition rows are still sitting unconsumed and **will fire on the next rotation that has an empty queue.** Fix shape: invert to (1) resolve winner → (2) apply disposition → (3) activate THAT person's queued game → (4) else pick window → timeout → runner-up → timeout → auto-pick. Genuine restructure of `processSlotMaintenance` (multi-slot, re-run guards, announcement copy all key off the current order) — wants tests, deliberately deferred. **ALSO FOUND: the Steam importer has NEVER imported a Retro or EM+ table** — `steamPinballPackContents.ts` contains ZERO of either; 28 `% Retro` rows and 8 `%EM+%` rows exist and ALL are AtGames-only (0 carry the Steam `zaccaria` platform, of 99 that do). Owner learned Steam Zaccaria sells them hidden inside Retro/EM+ packs → **~36 tables missing from the Steam side and those packs aren't in the curated list at all** (hand-curated, "no defined go-forward path"). **OPEN:** 16 remaining thin-duplicate merges (Zoo Keeper→Zoo Keeper (Pinball), Arkanoid, Cat in the Hat, Trolls Pinball, Adventures of Lara Croft, Zombie… all no-manufacturer rows folding into their AtGames twin; `Battle Deluxe (Combat Deluxe)`-style cases need longest-match) · 8 ambiguous name collisions left untouched (Circus vs Gottlieb/Bally/Exhibit, Whirlwind vs Gottlieb `Whirl-Wind`, Top Hand, Wizard, Hot Wheels, Black Hole, Space Shuttle vs Taito do Brasil/Unidesa) · manufacturer-PREFERENCE tie-break (the eStore knows the licensed brand for 189 rows; passing it as a tie-break only — NOT as `input.manufacturer` — would have put Time Machine on Zaccaria first time) · player-facing feature search SHELVED to ROADMAP (availability lives in `features`; neither `GET /api/global/games` nor the Library renders/filters features, which is why the owner read a `vpxs_manual` row as untagged — 283 rows carry `vpxs`, 625 `vpxs_manual`). **Backups on prod to delete when satisfied:** `arcaid.db.pre-atgames-cleanup`, `.pre-dup-repair`, `.pre-resync`, `.pre-timemachine`, `.pre-combat`.
- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-16 #74 (ATGAMES STUDIO COVERAGE 86% → 99%; branch `feature/atgames-studio-overrides`, follow-up to the MERGED PR #233; supersedes #73).** **PR #233 IS MERGED** (`8b8e9499d`, merge-commit style) — the AtGames API importer + `studio`/`atgames_id` are on main at v2.112.0, CI green, main verified building clean. **This follow-up closes the 39 unattributed tables the owner asked about.** **KEY DISCOVERY: most of that residue was a PARSER miss, not missing data.** Re-probing the store found (a) **Williams™ Pinball Volume 6 lists its tables as marketing copy** — `FUNHOUSE: Starring Rudy, pinball's most iconic ventriloquist dummy antagonist!` — so the whole sentence became the "table name"; new `stripBlurb` recovers the ALL-CAPS head (FunHouse / Space Station / Dr. Dude), and the ALL-CAPS requirement is the SAFETY property: ordinary colon titles (`Star Trek™ Pinball: Deep Space Nine`, `The Getaway: High Speed II`) have lowercase before the colon and are untouched — truncating one of those would silently collapse three distinct tables into a shared prefix (test-pinned); (b) **`Rare Pinball Pack` [AtGames Originals] lists "Battetoads™" — a typo in AtGames' own store**; (c) **Zaccaria Pinball Pack 7 lists "Zombie", API says "Zombies"**; (d) Primal Carnage is sold as `Zaccaria Primal Carnage Solid State` [Magic Pixel]. These naming divergences were left as OVERRIDES not fuzzy matches on purpose: a matcher able to bridge Battetoads→Battletoads could also bridge two genuinely different Zaccaria designs. **`src/services/atgamesStudioOverrides.ts` — 37 entries, STUDIO ONLY** (never manufacturer/year — VPS/OPDB know those better and a curated guess would compete with a real source), each carrying a `why` recording the store evidence; a test asserts every entry has one >20 chars, that no two entries claim the same table, and that no entry duplicates what a derived tier already owns (dead-weight guard). **Consulted LAST**, after pack-list → series → prefix → brand, so if AtGames ever adds a contents list to one of those packs the store wins automatically and the entry goes inert. **DRY-RUN (live): 279 pinball → 208 pack · 12 series · 12 prefix · 8 brand · 37 override · 2 none = 99%.** Zen 70 · Magic Pixel 145 · AtGames Originals 40 · FarSight 22. Zero dead override entries. **NOW 100% — 279/279 (39 override entries).** The final two, `City Golf` and `Wild Games`, were resolved by the OWNER supplying AtGames' published Originals release schedule (screenshot, 2026-08-16): both are AtGames Originals, **unreleased at the time** (10 Sep / 24 Sep 2026), alongside `Firefighter: Urban` (20 Aug) which corroborates the sibling-inference I'd already made. **THAT IS THE REAL LESSON AND IT'S NOW DOCUMENTED IN THE CODE: AtGames provisions a table into the catalogue FEED WEEKS BEFORE IT HAS A STORE LISTING**, so the unattributed WARN will fire mainly for announced-but-unreleased tables, NOT for storefront gaps — the fix is an interim override entry from the release schedule, which goes inert by itself once the table ships and a derived tier picks it up. Deliberately NOT pre-added: `Natural History Pinball Pack 4` (Dec 2026), `American Moments Pinball Bundle` (TBA), `First Responders: EMS` (Feb 2027) — not in the feed yet, so an entry would be an untestable guess. **ANSWER TO THE OWNER'S "is this future-proof?" QUESTION: the override MAP is not (it's a snapshot), but the SYSTEM is self-announcing** — the four derived tiers generalise (a future `Star Wars Pinball Legends Mini Pack` naming tables `Star Wars Pinball: <x>` is attributed with zero code change), and **every sync now logs a WARN naming the unattributed tables**, which is the documented maintenance trigger for the override file. Expect that WARN to always list City Golf + Wild Games; anything else in it means a new unenumerated pack. Tests 26 in `catalogue-atgames-identity.test.ts`. **STILL OPEN (unchanged from #73):** `resolveForTagging` has zero prod callers · `/admin/catalogue` has no edit form · `PinnedCarousel.tsx:184-190` CI flake root cause.
- **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-16 #73 (ATGAMES API IMPORTER BUILT — branch `feature/atgames-api-importer`, NOT yet merged or deployed; supersedes #72).** **The sheet-fed AtGames importer is replaced by AtGames' own public API + their Shopify storefront, and every table now carries a `studio`.** **Version bumped 2.111.0 → 2.112.0**; the CHANGELOG 2.112.0 section carries this arc AND a late "Catalogue" entry recording the `dedup_aliases` work under 2.111.0 that shipped without one (the ⚠ from #72 is now CLOSED). **What shipped in the branch:** (1) **migration 148** adds `global_games.atgames_id` (partial UNIQUE index) + `global_games.studio`, as a HANDLER (mirrors 133) so a failed ALTER halts startup instead of being swallowed; (2) `atgames_id` joins the step-1 external-id set, `hasExternalIdConflict`, and the `merge` data union — **in merge it is released from the SOURCE row first**, because unlike opdb/vps/igdb it carries a UNIQUE index and the target UPDATE runs while the source still exists; (3) **`AtGamesApiClient`** — cursor walk (`after`/`nextAfter`, 8 rows/page, ~50 requests for the current 404-row feed); (4) **`AtGamesEStoreClient`** — atgames.us is stock Shopify, so seven per-publisher collection handles served as `/collections/<h>/products.json` give the studio with no scraping; the pack→table mapping is the "Tables included" `<ul>` inside each product's `body_html`; (5) **`GlobalGameService.fillMissingManufacturer`** — gap-fill only, deliberately OUTSIDE the upsert input. **THREE FINDINGS THAT CHANGED THE DESIGN, all verified live 2026-08-16:** (a) **the API feed MIXES pinball and arcade** (first row is "8 Eyes") and `catalog`/`rule`/`table`/`table_rule`/`limit`/`page` are ALL silently ignored — only `keyword`/`prefix`/`after` work; the importer classifies on four pinball-EXCLUSIVE cabinet codes (`RK9920` Pinball 4K, `HA9919` HDP, `HA8818` Micro, `HA8819C` ES), verified 283 pinball / 121 arcade with no crossover — shared codes can't discriminate because an arcade ROM lists `HA8819` "Legends Pinball" too; (b) **the live feed carries AtGames' own test fixtures** "ZZZ Test 2/3/4/8" on pinball hardware — excluded, they would have imported as 4 junk rows; (c) **`vendor` is NOT the publisher** (it is "AtGames E-Store" on every product) — the storefront's Publisher facet is a metafield invisible to `products.json`, which is why the per-publisher COLLECTIONS are the door. **STUDIO ≠ MANUFACTURER is the load-bearing doctrine** (FarSight publishes Gottlieb, Magic Pixel publishes Zaccaria, Zen publishes Williams): putting a studio in `manufacturer` makes `manufacturerYearAgree` compare "magic pixel" to "zaccaria", fails step-4 dedup, and INSERTS a duplicate on every sync — the exact class the 88-row prod merge just repaired; "Zen Studios" would additionally trip `isVirtualOnlyManufacturer` and suppress IPDB merges. Test-pinned in `catalogue-atgames-identity.test.ts` (20 tests). **DRY-RUN NUMBERS against live prod-facing sources (no DB writes):** 404 feed rows → 279 pinball after test-row exclusion; **studio 240/279 = 86%** (205 pack-list · 12 series · 12 pack-title prefix · 11 announced brand) → Magic Pixel 142, Zen 46, AtGames Originals 30, FarSight 22; **manufacturer candidates 189** (Zaccaria 142, Williams 25, Gottlieb 22). **THE 39 UNATTRIBUTED are all in Zen and TAITO multi-packs the store does not enumerate** — Zen's Williams Volume tables without the brand prefix (Medieval Madness, Monster Bash, Junk Yard, Whirlwind, Swords of Fury, The Getaway, Bride of Pin·Bot, Creature from the Black Lagoon), Zen's licensed originals from the Universal/mini packs (Jaws, Godzilla, Borderlands, Homeworld, Jurassic Park, Knight Rider, E.T., Back to the Future, Xena, Battlestar Galactica, Brothers in Arms, Primal Carnage ×2, Wild Games, City Golf, Verne's Mysterious Island, Wrath of the Elder Gods, Zombies, Firefighter: Urban, A Samurai's Vengeance), and the TAITO Pinball Pack 2/3 set (Arkanoid, Bubble Bobble, Chack'n Pop, Elevator Action, The Legend of Kage, Operation Wolf, Rainbow Islands, Zoo Keeper). They keep a NULL studio rather than a guess — **owner call pending: add a committed override map (same convention as `steamPinballPackContents.ts`) to close them to 100%?** **STUDIO WRITE SEMANTICS ARE SUPPLIED-WINS** (`COALESCE(?, studio)`): a re-sync picks up a re-filing at the source, a null input never clears it, and an admin correction IS reverted by the next sync — fix attribution upstream, not in the row. **STILL OPEN FROM #72, NOT DONE HERE:** (1) `GlobalGameService.resolveForTagging` STILL has zero production callers — this importer resolves by `atgames_id` instead, so the exactly-one-candidate guard remains dormant; (2) `/admin/catalogue` STILL has no edit form (`PUT /api/admin/catalogue/games/:id` exists and is a safe partial update); (3) `PinnedCarousel.tsx:184-190` CI flake root cause unfixed (deterministic fix = `tabIndex={-1}` in the duplicated cards' JSX, delete the `useEffect` sweep). **NEXT STEPS:** owner reviews → PR → merge → deploy → click "Sync AtGames" on `/admin/catalogue` and check the sync log line (it reports created/updated/excluded + the studio tier breakdown). **Boxart contact sheet for all 404 API games** (owner asked how to view them; `boxart`/`boxart_480w` are plain public PNGs on assets.atgames.net) at `…/scratchpad/atgames-boxart.html`.

Row fields: `game_id`, `name`, `type` (`AtgLocalGame`), `hardware_models[]`, `internal_number`, `boxart`, `boxart_480w`, `external`, `rankings[]`. Model codes decode via `GET /api/arcadenet/d2d/arcade/v2/hardware_models` → 16 rows with `name` (`HA8800`), `display_name` (`Legends Ultimate (HA8800), 2019`), `series_name` — this REPLACES the hand-maintained sheet columns H/I/J/K. **The prize is `game_id`: it makes AtGames dedup an EXTERNAL-ID match (hierarchy step 1) instead of a fuzzy-name match (step 4), which is the root cause of everything this arc just cleaned up.** **The sheet is provably stale/incomplete** — the API carries `Locomotion Deluxe` (50687) and `Aerobatics Deluxe` (50682), both of which the sheet omits, which is exactly why the merge script reported them as "no AtGames twin"; API names also match the Zaccaria four-design taxonomy verbatim (`Circus` / `Circus 2017` / `Circus Retro`, `Locomotion` / `Locomotion 2018` / `Locomotion Deluxe` / `Locomotion Retro`). **TWO GAPS TO CLOSE IN THAT SAME ARC: (1) `GlobalGameService.resolveForTagging` (the exactly-one-candidate guard shipped by PR #232) has ZERO production callers — grep-verified, only its own definition at `GlobalGameService.ts:428`. - **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-08-11 #55 (v2.100.4 SHIPPED + PROD-VERIFIED — iOS PHOTO TRUNCATION FIX; supersedes #54).** PR #206 (`e0e84cbd9`), no migration. The v2.100.2 logging PAID OFF ON FIRST RETEST: SoggyBacon + owner's wife's iPhone both hit "unexpected end of form" — prod log shows 4× `Upload rejected ... Unexpected end of form {"mimetype":"unknown","size":"0"}` on Zankor Retro submit. REAL root cause (retrospective: the original report was likely NEVER HEIC — same truncation, previously an unlogged 500): iOS Safari camera-roll Files are LAZY disk references read only during upload streaming; the read can end early → multipart truncates before the photo bytes arrive → busboy "Unexpected end of form". Fix: `photoNormalize.normalizePhotoFile` MATERIALIZES every selected photo into an in-memory File at pick time (arrayBuffer → fresh File; converted path already in-memory; failure falls back to original) + `withUploadErrors` translates the raw busboy message to an actionable one (raw still logged). Tests: backend upload file 8 (+1), admin-ui **666** (materialization assertions replaced the pass-through pin). **AWAITING iPhone retest round 3** — this fix targets the OBSERVED failure, not the hypothesized one; if it recurs the log line will say so. - **▶ RESUME POINT — 2026-07-31 #17 (v2.58.0 + v2.59.0 shipped — ADR 0016 phases 3 and 4 complete; P2 is specced and NOT started; supersedes #16 for "what's next").** Continues the **Scoreboard Appearance Overhaul** program. **Durable docs: `docs/decisions/0016-engine-device-score-provenance.md` (committed, and AMENDED this session). ⚠️ Phase contracts live in `tmp/*.md` — LOCAL-ONLY, not in git. ADR 0016 + these markers are the real handoff.**
  - **v2.58.0 (PR #140) — ADR 0016 P3: reads move onto engine + device, fidelity categories arrive.** Reads had still been going through the legacy `platform` column. Two live bugs fixed in passing: `LeaderboardService.getForGameByPlatform` compared raw strings with no alias folding while `getDistinctPlatforms` *did* fold — so a tab could be labelled from folded data and then query a value matching zero rows; and the catalogue `platforms` filter was substring-matching raw JSON, so `LIKE '%vpx%'` also matched `vpxs`. Categories (**Real · Simulation · Arcade-Style · Video Games**) derive from **engine alone, never device**; `unknown` yields *no* category and renders "Unspecified". `?platform=` survives as a deprecated alias.
  - **v2.59.0 (PR #141) — ADR 0016 P4: per-category cards.** The request that started the arc. `GROUP BY gg.id` → `GROUP BY gg.id, <category>`; the category `CASE` is **generated from `CANONICAL_ENGINES` at query-build time** (`src/utils/engineCategorySql.ts`) so SQL never becomes a fourth taxonomy copy. **The trap that was caught before it shipped:** 38 of 67 prod global scores are `engine='unknown'`, so cards for only the three known bands would have silently dropped the MAJORITY of scores off the site — hence the visible, deliberately-not-a-peer **Unspecified** bucket, with a test asserting a game's cards contain every one of its scores exactly once. Pagination needed a real fix too: `gg.name` was never unique and is now doubly non-unique, so every sort ends in `(gg.id, category)`.
  - **✅ PROD-VERIFIED 2026-07-31** — `/api/version` reports **v2.59.0 @ `43a6ba7d1`** (the #141 merge), container healthy. `/api/global/scoreboard` confirms P4 live: rows carry `category` + `card_id`, **3 games in the first 100 rows render more than one card**, and all five buckets are populated (`simulation` 10, `unspecified` 23, `real` 2, `arcade_style` 3, plus 62 `(null)` = zero-score games showing their single uncategorised `Claim 1st →` card, exactly as specced). **Nothing to re-check on resume.**
  - **DECISION RECORDED — iScored scores never reach the Global Scoreboard (user, 2026-07-31).** Now written into **ADR 0016** under *"iScored-synced scores never reach the Global Scoreboard"* — that section, not this marker, is the durable copy. Three rules: sync stays **tournament-only** (already structurally true — `ScoreSyncPoller.findLocalGameForIscoredId` INNER JOINs `tournaments`, so pinned rows are unreachable; locked by test, not rebuilt); **classify where the data supports it** (tournament rules permitting exactly one engine → that engine; else an admin-declared per-tournament default; else `unknown`); and **excluded from `global_scores` unconditionally**, including successfully-classified rows. Prod evidence that forced it: all 6 `source='sync'` rows are `unknown`, and 5 of 37 games with global scores already show a real card beside an Unspecified one. **Accepted cost, stated to the user:** rooms running entirely through iScored contribute nothing to the Global Scoreboard.
  - **▶ NEXT: P2 — the last and riskiest ADR 0016 phase. Contract written, reviewed by nobody, NOT started: `tmp/engine-device-p2-contract.md`.** It folds the iScored work above into the tournament-rules rework. **The hazard that shapes the whole contract:** `platform_rules` is parsed at **10 independent sites and 8 of them swallow malformed JSON into "no rules"** — which means *a tournament that restricts nothing*. `rooms.ts:770` and `:777` parse the same row twice. So the contract mandates a boring first commit (centralise all 10 behind one `parseTournamentRules` that logs instead of failing silently) **before** the shape changes, plus one test per site. Second mandate: **~200 live rooms have tournaments in the legacy flat rule shape — read-time shim, no destructive migration, no clean break.** The legacy→two-axis lift table is the piece most likely to be subtly wrong and is invisible in the diff; require it be spelled out in the implementer's report.
  - **Session housekeeping:** CHANGELOG had **two independent `## [2.59.0]` entries** (both written this arc, each carrying details the other lacked) — merged into one. Test baselines re-verified on `main` and corrected in the P2 contract: **backend 951, admin-ui 263** (I had written 262, which would have let a test be deleted unnoticed).
  - **SETTLED — wipe ALL unknown-engine scores (user, 2026-07-31).** "There are no 'real scores' in Arcaid yet. It is not GA. You can wipe whatever score you want without consequence." This overrides my earlier recommendation to preserve the AtGames rows as genuinely-ambiguous-rather-than-missing — that caution assumed the data had value it does not yet have. **Fold the wipe into P2 as a migration** (not an ad-hoc prod query) so it applies deterministically and is tracked: delete unknown-engine rows from `score_history`, `submissions`, `community_scores` and `global_scores`. Do it **after** P2's derivation lands, so rows that P2 can now classify are re-stamped rather than deleted. Pre-GA licence applies to score data only — it is not licence to skip the FK/unlink discipline in `database.ts`.
  - **SETTLED — no inference for iScored scores at all (user, 2026-07-31). Nothing open on this arc now.** The user rejected the derive-from-tournament-rules design outright: *"I don't think we should try and determine or infer where a score was played on at all if the score comes from iScored… It's more of a stop gap during migrations."* So synced scores are **always `unknown`/`unknown`**, are excluded from `global_scores`, and a player who wants a score on the global board enters it in Arcaid. **This SHRANK P2** — the derivation logic, the per-tournament default admin UI, the save-time validation and the backfill action are all deleted from the contract, and `tournaments.iscored_default_engine`/`_device` become vestigial (stop reading them; leave the columns, SQLite drops need a table rebuild). Recorded in ADR 0016; the ADR is the durable copy.
  - **Two iScored import paths, not one — found while specc'ing the above.** `ScoreSyncPoller` (continuous) **and** `TournamentEngine.finalSyncScoresForGame` (~line 415, final sync on deactivate/delete). Both read the now-vestigial default columns; only the poller fans out to global. A change that only touched the poller would have left the other path reading dead config.
  - **⚠️ Do NOT delete the Global Scoreboard's "Unspecified" card when it goes empty.** After P2 wipes the unknown rows and keeps synced scores out, the only remaining source of an `unknown` *global* row is a web submission — and engine is required at the API boundary — so the bucket likely never renders on the global board again. It stays, with its tests and the coverage invariant, as a **safety net**: P4's whole point was that scores with no matching card silently vanish from the site, and removing the net because it is currently unused is precisely how that returns. Room-level surfaces still render Unspecified for real, since synced scores keep landing in `score_history`.
  - **ROADMAP additions from this session (all present, lines 9–13):** websocket per-card bump wart · no navigation out of the room admin area (+ make `BrandWordmark` link to `/` by default) · searchable Personal Bests on the Stats page · catalogue still describes legacy platforms rather than engines + device availability · iScored provenance (now marked RESOLVED-IN-DESIGN, folded into P2).

  - **v2.54.0 (PR #134) — username lock + identity-resolved reads.** Re-implementation of a stale PR #130 (closed as superseded) rather than a rebase: it predated four releases and collided semantically in five core files, with one hunk having no anchor left. Field bug: a logged-in user typed a fake name and saw their real name on the leaderboard but the fake one on the ticker/history — leaderboards join `user_profiles`, history/community reads shipped raw `iscored_username`. Fix: `UserProfileService.resolveSubmitName` (room claim → profile display_name → JWT claim → raw id); all four submit routes discard posted names for authed users (**guests unchanged** — free-text + first-claim-wins); global alias claim can now only register a canonical name (**pre-lock ANY typed name became a permanent account alias**). Two improvements over #130: `displayName` **omitted** from the global schema (declared-and-ignored still 400s a >50-char value; `z.object` strips unknown keys) and the client omits `username` entirely for authed users (#130 still gated Submit on a local name → a corrupt stored session left Submit permanently disabled with no field to fix it).
  - **v2.55.0 (PR #135) — full-card pinned carousel + header + light logo.** `GameCard`/`LeaderboardRow` extracted to `GlobalGameCard` so grid and rail render the SAME component ("look the same" is structural, not a copy). Carousel auto-cycles **only when measured to overflow**; pauses on hover/focus/touch (these cards have Submit buttons — unlike the landing ticker's decorative tiles); reduced-motion disables it; duplicated cards `aria-hidden` + non-focusable; mobile is scroll-snap swipe with **no** auto-advance. New `--color-brand-delta` token (the logo's exact pink — neon-magenta hue 354 and neon-coral hue 13 sit either side of it and both read visibly wrong beside the mark). Header centred + 1.5x from `sm:` up (mobile keeps original sizes — 45px wraps to two lines on a 390px phone). Light logo wired: **1x asset over the package's recommended 2x** (~187px display width → 1x is still 3.7x density, saves ~380KB); header logo 64→88px because the artwork specs a 180px minimum width and ships no wordmark-only fallback. `.theme-light --color-deep` → **#E8EAF0** (the artwork's design canvas). ⚠️ **Vertical cost accepted, not solved:** pins section 167→454px, grid barely above the fold at 1440x900.
  - **v2.56.0 (PR #138) — winner-picks is per-tournament only + landing light mode.** **Field bug, root-caused on prod:** a tournament with winner-picks ✓ and a 60-min window auto-picked instantly. `PickAwardGate` resolved `room AND tournament`, and room-level `ENABLE_GAME_PICK_AWARD` was **absent → false**, so three correct tournament settings were inert; `tournamentWin` isn't gated by it but `turnToPick` is → "congratulated, then nothing". The key appeared **only** in `Settings.tsx` — `TournamentForm` never referenced it, so the form showed live-looking dead controls. **User decision: room toggle removed.** Verified blast radius: 5 rooms, one set it (`'true'`), four absent, **none `'false'`**, all 9 tournaments `winner_picks=1` → four rooms began honouring winner-picks. Room-scoped callers (Picks tab, Mystery Award, admin enablement) now resolve as **"any tournament in this room has winner_picks"** — NOT blanket true, or a room with it off everywhere renders live controls that do nothing (the exact failure removed). Class doc rewritten — it documented the deleted override rule in confident detail and was much of why this was invisible. Migration 126 drops the rows. Landing light mode: ticker tiles were **entirely inline-styled dark with no tokens** (why they never followed the theme) → 18 tokens, zero literal rgba, `PlayerAvatar` replaces the local hue-hash chip (**changes the dark avatar palette** — consequence of the requested swap); hero renders the light PNG in light polarity (**accepted loss: no glitch animation in light** — no animated light source shipped); motto `#6427af`, measured **7.12:1** on #E8EAF0.
  - **v2.57.0 (PR #139) — hero card, density toggle, live indicator (A5a).** **A5 was split**; A5b (Grid/Compact layout + rank alerts) is deliberately isolated. **Deliberate design deviation:** the handoff crowns "most scores in trailing 7 days" with a HOT badge, but prod's trailing week is one game at 5 scores and three at 1 — literally applied, a quiet week stamps HOT on a single-score game and the design's own criterion ("genuinely trending, not just the first row") fails. `HERO_MIN_WEEKLY_SCORES=3`: above → HOT + weekly delta; below → top `score_count` in the **same filtered view**, `is_hot:false` gating BOTH badge and weekly line; nothing scored → `hero:null`; `offset>0` omits the key. `buildCatalogueFilters()` extracted so hero and grid derive filters from one place. Density toggle flips **client-side with zero refetch** (that's why `neighbors` shipped early in A4); test asserts the call count is unchanged. Persisted under the **namespaced** `global_density` key — `/api/me/preferences` is theme-only/admin-scoped and would 400. Player-count line omitted (no API).
  - **Dependency bumps:** #123 (admin-ui minor/patch) merged green. **#124 jsdom 30 and #89 TypeScript 7 both merged then REVERTED** (#136, #137) — jsdom 30 dies at `webidl.util.markAsUncloneable is not a function` (all 31 FE test files fail to start a worker); TS7 fails `npm ci` on `typescript-eslint@8.65.0` peer range `<6.1.0`. **Both need a coordinated bump, not a solo one** — diagnosis recorded on each PR. #122's Playwright bump required a same-PR Dockerfile base-image bump (verified by actually launching chromium in the prod container, since version agreement on paper proves nothing).

One Arcaid hub server that players join unlocks DMs for every standalone room, no code. Web push already covers `turnToPick`. Also open: proof photos too buried on room score cards (ROADMAP, user deferred until the UX arc lands); landing `RoomCard`/`CreateRoomCard` still inline-styled dark.

Owns the page's existing field; ↑↓ wrap, ↵ submits, ⌘↵ opens detail, Esc returns focus. **Never `scrollIntoView`** (scrolls the page out from under it). Search learned year tokens + AND-across-terms; a 4-digit token counts as a year only in 1900-2099, and a year **widens** rather than narrows (else *Pinball 2000* [a 1999 title] and *1942* stop matching). Manufacturer matching already existed — the handoff was wrong.

Migration 124. **Handoff FK was invalid** (`global_games(global_game_id)`; PK is `id`) — would have migrated clean and failed on first pin, so the test INSERTS. `/global/scoreboard` got `optionalDiscordUser` (it was fully public; `requireAuth` would have killed anonymous browsing); **anonymous payload byte-identical**, asserted. Pin lookup is a correlated subquery not a LEFT JOIN (the query groups by game — a join inflates `score_count`). Plus two user-reported fixes: platform pills got a semi-opaque fill (6% tint vanished on busy art), room badges moved to sit right after the username (the name span's `flex-1` was flinging them right).
  - **v2.53.0 (PR #133, ADR 0016 Phase 1)** — **engine + device score provenance.** `platform` conflated *engine* (decides comparability) with *device* (provenance); AtGames runs VPX/FX/Zaccaria/native so one field can't work. **User rulings: VPX Standalone + vpxs_manual are the VPX engine** (ports with graphical concessions; physics/rulesets unchanged) **and vpxs_manual is always device=atgames**; **BAM is NOT an engine** (it entered via `VPS_FORMAT_MAP` because VPS emits it as a table format — it's a catalogue tag, `fp` on the score axis). VR ids dissolve (`fx_vr` → engine `fx` + device `vr_headset`). Migration 125, **prod-verified**: 63 rows → `unknown`/`atgames` (the irreducible AtGames ambiguity, >half of all scores), 25 → unknown/unknown, 18 vpx, 5 vpx/atgames, zero NULLs, case-splits collapsed. **`'unknown'` is a first-class value, never NULL.** Sharp catches worth preserving: naive COALESCE-preserve would be WRONG on upsert (`'unknown'` is a value, so a sync tick clobbers a real engine) → all 6 sites use `COALESCE(NULLIF(excluded.engine,'unknown'), …)`; and the parity test asserts every legacy mapping yields a **self-compatible** pair, else migration writes rows the validator rejects. `ensurePlatformAllowed`'s null-means-allowed replaced by a discriminated union. Also fixed: 4 writers that dropped platform, 2 draft-commit paths that never validated, and Discord/web resolving different valid sets for room-tagged games.

Naming decided: pinball fidelity is **Arcade-Style** and the video group renames to **Video Games**, because `arcade` is already a live platform id.

First found (container tests) that sqlite3 6.0.1's linux-x64 glibc prebuild needs `GLIBC_2.38`+`GLIBCXX_3.4.31`, which the jammy prod base (glibc 2.35) lacks. Migration: prod base `jammy`→`noble` (Ubuntu 24.04, glibc 2.39) + Node 20→24 (noble ships Node 24, pinned via `setup_24.x`) + sqlite3 6 + new `.dockerignore`. **Validated exhaustively pre-merge:** sqlite3 6 + Node 24 + real DB open on the actual noble base ✅; full multi-stage image build ✅; **full app boot on the built image ✅** (`/api/status` online, db ok, ALL migrations ran, poller+WS+API up, `node v24.18.0`); CI gate ✅. Merged #45 → deployed → **prod 502'd ~4 min (failed to boot)** → **reverted** (`git revert -m 1`, `0355509ca`) → prod recovered on the jammy/sqlite3-5/Node-20 image (online, db ok, 1 room). **ROOT-CAUSE GAP: the full-boot validation used a FRESH EMPTY DB with NO credentials — it never exercised the prod-only boot path**: decrypting real `SECRETS_KEY`-encrypted Discord/iScored settings at "load settings" (runs BEFORE the API starts → a failure there = exactly the 502 seen), the real populated DB, the bind-mounted `/app/data` volume + `arcaid` UID (noble may assign a different UID than jammy), and real Discord/iScored connects. Prod DB NOT modified (crash was pre-write; backup taken regardless). **DIAGNOSIS PENDING** the crashed container's `docker logs arcaid` (requested from user). **A re-attempt MUST boot-test against a COPY of the prod DB (real encrypted settings + SECRETS_KEY + the mounted-volume/UID setup), not a fresh DB.** **→ RESOLVED + SHIPPED (PR #46, deployed 2026-07-05).** Reproduced the crash locally against a COPY of the prod DB under real Linux perms (Path B): it was NOT the decrypt path (Discord connected fine on Node 24 against the real DB) — a **UID mismatch**. noble's `useradd` gives `arcaid` **uid 997**, but prod's `/app/data`+`arcaid.db` are owned by **uid 999** (jammy's arcaid) and the CMD's `/app/data` chown is non-recursive → 997 can't write the 999-owned DB → `SQLITE_READONLY` at bootstrap → 502. Test A (997)→crash reproduced; Test B (999)→clean boot. **FIX: pin `arcaid` to uid 999** (`useradd -u 999`). Shipped via **#46** (clean re-attempt off main = full migration + the uid pin); gate green; deployed; **prod ONLINE on noble/Node24/sqlite3-6** (db ok read-write, 1 room, Discord+iScored ok, uptime climbing). No prod-side chown needed (files already 999-owned; the failed container crashed pre-write). CLAUDE.md documents the uid-999 gotcha + the fresh-DB-test-insufficient lesson. **Dependabot backlog 16/16 COMPLETE.**

DEVIATION: websocket room-scoping deferred to S4 (scoping `emitLeaderboardUpdated` in isolation would deliver to zero clients — no FE joins a `room:` channel until S4's socket wiring).

Migration **104** — orphan cleanup + `game_room_game_library` rebuild (drops dead `game_library` FK from migration 092, columns preserved via PRAGMA table_info). 4 delete paths fixed (admin remove-game, TournamentService/GameRoomService/GlobalGameService.delete). Audited via a 6-agent workflow that reproduced 4 live breaks. CLAUDE.md Database section updated.

- **Phase A / S5 — The submit moment — COMPLETE 2026-06-15, on PR #5 (`phase-a-s5-submit-moment`, off main), NOT yet merged:**
  - **BE canonical-partition rank helper** (`src/services/ScoreRankService.ts`, NEW): `computeRoomRank`/`computeGlobalRank` return `{rank,totalPlayers,previousBest,gapToNext,gapToFirst}`, best-effort (return an all-null object on error, NEVER fail the already-committed insert). Partition by the canonical `COALESCE(submitted_by_user_id,'iscored:'||LOWER(iscored_username))` (room) / 3-arg global variant — **verified char-for-char against LeaderboardService/GlobalLeaderboardService** so multi-alias users get a rank that agrees with the board. The inline `LobbyFeedGenerator` rank logic (wrong `LOWER(iscored_username)`-only partition) is LEFT UNTOUCHED — its rework belongs to S6. Wired into `CommunityScoreService.submitScore` + `global.ts` POST /global/scores. `scoreRank.test.ts` (7 tests incl. alias-collapse).
  - **FE result card** replaces the 1.2s auto-close toast (`SubmissionSheet.tsx`): persistent "You're #N of M / X behind #1 / new-high-score" card + View-leaderboard link + Done; guest-conversion CTA every 3rd guest submit; mobile keyboard fix (`inputMode='none'` on touch suppresses the OS keyboard so the in-app one drives input) + Shift key on `OnScreenKeyboard`. QR submit (`ScoreSubmit.tsx`) deep-links to `/:slug/games/:name?highlight=<name>`; `GameDetail` consumes `?highlight=` (rings + scrolls the row) and defaults to the leaderboard tab when tournament data exists. `PublicStats` search matches `display_name`. `Picks` guest-login banner with returnPath.

SW `CACHE_NAME` → **v71**.

The rank correctly matches `LeaderboardService.recalculate` (the tournament board).

- **Phase A / S6 — Dethrone loop & notification hygiene — COMPLETE 2026-06-15, on PR #23 (`phase-a-s6-dethrone-loop`, off main), NOT yet merged:**
  - **Dethrone DM revived** (`LobbyFeedGenerator.ts`): decoupled from the cosmetic `new_high_score` feed toggle (was silently gated by it — a room disabling that feed killed the retention DM); deep-links to the GAME (`/:slug/games/:name`) not the room root; includes the margin ("beating you by X" / "tying your top score"); passes `roomId` so the per-room `DISCORD_ENABLED` gate applies (was bypassed).
  - **`tournamentStarting` room-scoped** (`Scheduler.resolveTournamentStartingRecipients`): DMs only the tournament room's `room_members` (was ALL opted-in users globally → cross-room spam); legacy NULL-room tournaments keep the global fan-out as documented back-compat.
  - **Rate-limit fairness** (`NotificationService`): separate per-class budgets (`${userId}:high` vs `:chatty`, 5/hr each) so chatty types (turnToPick/friendScore/tournamentStarting) can't starve `rankDethroned`/`tournamentWin`.
  - **⚠️ FLAGGED default-ON flip — SHIPS INERT.** `rankDethroned`+`tournamentWin` default-ON for Discord-linked users is gated behind global SettingsService key **`NOTIFY_HIGH_VALUE_DEFAULT_ON`** — NO seed row → resolves OFF → zero behavior change on deploy. An explicit user pref (true OR false) always wins (the `explicitKeys` set distinguishes "absent" from "explicitly false"); rollback = set the key `false`. **TO ACTIVATE: set `NOTIFY_HIGH_VALUE_DEFAULT_ON=true`** (super-admin settings). Flag read cached 10s (propagation delay; `NotificationService.invalidateFlagCache()` exists but is NOT wired to settings writes). First flag-defaulted DM appends a one-time "manage via /arcaid-notifications or Account Settings" footer (`_hvFooterShown` marker in the user's prefs JSON).
  - **Milestones** (`MilestoneService` + **migration 105** `player_milestones_fired`): exact-equals → **crossed-threshold** detection backed by a UNIQUE-on-`(game_room_id, player_key, scope, threshold)` idempotency table (`INSERT OR IGNORE` + `changes()===1`); fires the threshold value (9→11 reads "10th"); uses resolved `displayName`. CAVEAT (out of S6 scope): counts stay alias-keyed while de-dup is canonical — multi-alias users may under-count totals, but no cross-alias double-fire.
  - **Web Notifications card** in `AccountSettings` — 5 toggles over the existing `GET/PUT /api/me/notification-preferences` (no new routes).
  - **Built via a 10-agent workflow** (recon → 3 file-partitioned implementers → integrate → 3 adversarial verify lenses); verdicts **safe / pass / pass**. **194/194 backend tests** (23 files, +2 S6 suites), admin-ui build + 2 tests clean. SW `CACHE_NAME` → **v72**.
